# Closed app shortcut request history

Snapshot: 22 October 2026. Baseline: `b856a45e684775fd477dbcd8d1342ff65bb97e38`. These 384 closed app-related issues supplement the [open request audit](APP_SHORTCUT_AUDIT.md) and [modernization plan](MODERNIZATION_PLAN.md). Historical closure, catalog presence, and old release announcements do not establish a working current launch. Recheck region, platform, app availability, and retirement before restoring a historical shortcut.

## Closed app requests and app-specific regression history

All closed shortcut-title/label requests are included, plus app-specific reports discovered through the complete inventory. Related open issues identify unresolved current reports even when the historical preset was implemented. The code cross-check reflects the audited local snapshot, not the code as it existed when the issue closed.

| No. | Issue → app / platform | Disposition and implementation scope | Requested metadata (not proof of launch) | Local code cross-check | Reported working launch / acceptance |

## Evidence limitations

The audit proves inventory/comment coverage, request contents, local catalog/dispatch presence and the cited historical reports. It does not prove present-day availability of every service, installed package, exported activity, device connection, or successful app launch. Closed release confirmations are historical evidence; supplied component dumps remain candidates unless a successful launch is explicitly reported. Full request bodies and comments remain in the local audit evidence cache; this appendix preserves the relevant metadata, source links, dispositions, and acceptance requirements.
